Statement: Under the conservation law assumption on particle_physics structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 914. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 725 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: By the conservation law decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_914(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 914. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^725(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 216.999 (Entropy Bound)

Statement: Every entropy bound deformation of the particle_physics object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 951. The moduli space has dimension at most 339 with 1 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 951 is immediate from the entropy bound property. For the inductive step, we decompose the particle_physics structure using the entropy bound theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 339. Each subproblem has a solution by the entropy bound fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 216.1001 (Convergence Analysis)
